// Symbols seeded from the SLUS_209.11 IDB by tools/ida_symbols.py.
// Hand-curated symbols go above the generated block and win over it.
// .sdata's size (0x3355) is not a multiple of 4: it ends with the string
// ".ips" plus its terminator. spimdisasm emits whole words, so the odd last
// byte needs its own symbol or the image comes out one byte short.
D_00551B54 = 0x00551B54; // type:u8 size:0x1

// pbGame's cursor pair: gameKey1 addresses these as D_00551EC4 and
// D_00551EC4 + 0x2, so the 4-byte .sbss object splat emits is two s16s. With
// one symbol every spelling of the second half goes through the first's
// address, which leaves gameKey1 18 words out.
D_00551EC4 = 0x00551EC4; // type:s16 size:0x2
D_00551EC6 = 0x00551EC6; // type:s16 size:0x2
// datCalc.c's boss-press table: 40 x datBossPress_t, read by datBossRashChk
// (0x00246038 loads %hi/%lo of it) and datBossRash{GetRec,UpdRec}.
datBossPress = 0x003FA430; // size:0x280

// sdfPadInitManager reads two bytes per pad out of gp-0x28A0 (0x005518D0),
// stepping the index by 2, and passes them to scePadPortOpen as port and slot;
// the word there is 0x00010000, i.e. (port 0, slot 0) and (port 1, slot 0).
sdfPadPortSlotPairs = 0x005518D0; // type:u8 size:0x4

// The battle camera object the nbCamera* entry points operate on;
// nbCameraClearState zeroes +0x48..+0x54 of it. Named in the IDB.
pNbCamera = 0x00320E40;

// sdfFileSetDefaultSrc (0x002BE598) ends in a tail call to 0x002BE5B0, which
// only stores the byte in sdfFileCmdDefaultDevice: a separate static function.
sdfFileCmdSetDefaultDevice = 0x002BE5B0; // type:func size:0x8

// nbCameraBoss_Sp_MURET2_Run indexes two 0x38-byte bezier parts here, the
// target curve then the eye curve. The IDB splits them into interior fields:
// nbCameraMuret2Timer is [0].step, and nbCamera_MURET2_B4P and
// nbCameraMuret2State are [1].weight and [1].step.
D_0056B350 = 0x0056B350;

// effPadVibeFadeUpdate indexes the two per-controller frame counters.
D_0054F200 = 0x0054F200; // type:s32 size:0x8
D_0054F208 = 0x0054F208; // type:s32 size:0x8

// Retail accesses this ordering-table entry through its own address.
D_002F64B8 = 0x002F64B8; // size:0x20

// Facility script flags at gp-0x21F0, between trmUpdateWork and EventStart.
D_00551F80 = 0x00551F80;

// Debug selection-marker pointers follow the eight-byte ALPHA label.
D_0054F4D0 = 0x0054F4D0;

// RunProcess copies two 2-axis sticks per port and their status bytes.
dds3PadStickPrev = 0x0054E8F8; // type:u8 size:0x8
dds3PadStickStatusPrev = 0x0054E900; // type:s8 size:0x4
dds3PadStickState = 0x0054E908; // type:u8 size:0x8
dds3PadStickStatusState = 0x0054E910; // type:u8 size:0x4

// Native parallel-light interpolation timer extents.
lightcolornow = 0x00551BC0; // type:s16 size:0x8
lightcolormax = 0x00551BC8; // type:s16 size:0x8
lightdirnow = 0x00551BD0; // type:s16 size:0x8
lightdirmax = 0x00551BD8; // type:s16 size:0x6

// Eight globals the IDB names but tools/ida_symbols.py drops: IDA set these
// names itself, so has_user_name is false for them even though they are not
// dummy names. The code corroborates each one: mccb is the handle every
// sdfMc* call in slMain takes, MidiNo is what p2sdLoadCheckMidi and
// p2sdLoadSMGFile are given, and pointResName is the name fldSetSceneParam2
// receives. 0x00531F28 is left alone: IDA calls it FoceScript, but the bytes
// there are the string "elvstart_label".
pointResName = 0x0054EB30; // type:u8 size:0x8
hDistortionBlur = 0x0054EB5C; // type:u32 size:0x4
hPartialMultiBlur = 0x0054EC68; // type:u32 size:0x4
MidiNo = 0x0054EE40; // type:u32 size:0x4
hEffPart = 0x0054EF38; // type:u32 size:0x4
shutdownfunc = 0x0054FBB0; // type:u32 size:0x4
hCAMPBadEffect = 0x0054FEE8; // type:u32 size:0x4
mccb = 0x005516BC; // type:u32 size:0x4

// effBattle's packed battle-light colour word, written from the per-kind
// colour tables and read back by effBTLFieldColorGetBaseColor. The IDB names
// it, uniquely, but tools/ida_symbols.py drops it: IDA set the name itself, so
// has_user_name is false even though it is not a dummy name.
abgr = 0x00551D88; // type:u32 size:0x4

// nbSetBattleDummy takes the dummy record, two lists at +0xF0 and +0x100, and
// a skill list. TestProcess8 reaches its five through four separate symbols
// (D_005269D0, D_00526AC0, D_00526AD0, D_00526B48) and matches, so the 0x120
// the IDB gives test_skilllist and test2_pdummy spans three objects each, and
// one symbol folds three addresses into one lui/addiu pair.
D_00526530 = 0x00526530; // size:0x10
D_00526540 = 0x00526540; // size:0x20
D_005267D0 = 0x005267D0; // size:0x10
D_005267E0 = 0x005267E0; // size:0x20

// libdma's channel table, read by sceDmaGetChan (which returns an entry for
// channels 0..9) and sceDmaReset. Its ten words are the EE DMA channel
// register bases 0x10008000..0x1000D400, in channel-number order.
sceDmaChanRegs = 0x00529F70; // size:0x28
